{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}(\widetilde{S}_{t_{3}}\widetilde{S}_{t_{3}}^{\top}-\Sigma_{\star}^{2})\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t_{3}}\widetilde{S}_{t_{3}}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq\frac{\sqrt{r_{\star}}}{5}.

(163)

Combining the two inequalities (
162
) and (
163
) yields
for all
t\in[t_{3},T_{\max}]

\displaystyle{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}(\widetilde{S}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma_{\star}^{2})\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{5}\left(1-\frac{\eta}{10}\right)^{t-t_{3}}\sqrt{r_{\star}}+\frac{10C_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3-aux}}\kappa^{4}}{\|X_{\star}\|^{2}}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+10\max_{t_{3}\leq\tau\leq t}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{\tau}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/2}.

We can then invoke Lemma
41
to see that

\displaystyle{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}-M_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{4\|X_{\star}\|^{2}}{5}\left(1-\frac{\eta}{10}\right)^{t-t_{3}}\sqrt{r_{\star}}+10C_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3-aux}}\kappa^{4}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+40\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\max_{t_{3}\leq\tau\leq t}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{\tau}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/2}+4\|X_{\star}\|\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{10}\right)^{t-t_{3}}\sqrt{r_{\star}}\|M_{\star}\|+10C_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3-aux}}\kappa^{4}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+80\|M_{\star}\|\max_{t_{3}\leq\tau\leq t}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{\tau}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/2},

where in the last line we use
\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|\leq\|X_{\star}\|
—an implication of (
24
), which holds in the generalized setting by Lemma
31
.
To see this, the assumption (
12c
) implies that

\alpha\leq\|X_{\star}\|
as long as
\eta\leq 1/2
and
C_{\alpha}\geq 4
,
which in turn implies
\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|\leq\alpha^{2/3}\|X_{\star}\|^{1/3}\leq\|X_{\star}\|
.
This completes the proof for the first part of Lemma
40
with
c_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3}}=1/(10C_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3-aux}})
.

For the second part of Lemma
40
,
notice that

8c_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3}}^{-1}\max_{t_{3}\leq\tau\leq T_{\max}}(\|\widetilde{O}_{\tau}\|/\|X_{\star}\|)^{1/2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/3}

by (
24
), thus

{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}-M_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq(1-c_{\ref{lem:p3}}\eta)^{t-t_{3}}\sqrt{r_{\star}}\|M_{\star}\|+c_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3}}^{-1}\kappa^{4}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/3}

for
t_{3}\leq t\leq T_{\max}
.
There exists some iteration number

t_{4}:t_{3}\leq t_{4}\leq t_{3}+\frac{2}{c_{\ref{lem:noisy-p3}}\eta}\log(\|X_{\star}\|/\alpha)\leq t_{3}+T_{\min}/16

such that

(1-c_{\ref{lem:p3}}\eta)^{t_{4}-t_{3}}\leq\left(\frac{\alpha}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{r_{\star}}}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{1/3},

where the last inequality is due to (
12c
).
It is then clear that
t_{4}
has the property claimed in the lemma.

G.3.1
Proof of Lemma
42

The idea is the same as the proof of Lemma
28
.
Fix any
t\in[t_{3},T_{\max}]
, if (
161
) were true for all
\tau\in[t_{3},t]
,
taking into account that
\|\widetilde{O}_{\tau}\|/\|X_{\star}\|\leq 1/10000

for all
\tau\in[t_{3},T_{\max}]
by (
24
) (which still holds in the generalized setting according to Lemma
32
),
we could show by induction that
\|\Gamma_{\tau}\|\leq 1/10
for all
\tau\in[t_{3},t]
.
Thus it suffices to assume
\|\Gamma_{t}\|\leq 1/10
and prove (
161
).

Apply Lemma
39
to obtain

{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|{\Gamma_{t+1}}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq(1-\eta){|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|{\Gamma_{t}}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\eta\frac{C_{\ref{lem:local-unify}}\kappa^{4}}{\|X_{\star}\|^{2}}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|{U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta^{\prime}_{t}}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\frac{1}{16}\eta\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\eta\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{7/12},

(164)

In addition, Lemma
37
tells us that

{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t+1}\widetilde{S}_{t+1}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{3(\|Z_{t}\|+\eta)}\right){|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\eta\frac{C_{\ref{lem:misalign-update}}\kappa^{2}}{c_{\lambda}\|X_{\star}\|}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta^{\prime}_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\eta\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\sigma_{\min}(\widetilde{S}_{t})}\right)^{2/3}\|X_{\star}\|,

where
Z_{t}=\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}(\widetilde{S}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{\top}+\lambda I)\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}
.
It is easy to check that
\|Z_{t}\|\leq 1+\|\Gamma_{t}\|+c_{\lambda}\leq 2
as
\|\Gamma_{t}\|\leq 1/10
and
c_{\lambda}
is sufficiently small.
In addition, one has
\sigma_{\min}(\widetilde{S}_{t})^{2}\geq(1-\|\Gamma_{t}\|)\sigma_{\min}(X_{\star})^{2}

and
\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|/\sigma_{\min}(\widetilde{S}_{t})\leq(2\kappa)^{-24}
.
Combine these relationships together to arrive at

{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t+1}\widetilde{S}_{t+1}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{8}\right){|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\eta\frac{C_{\ref{lem:misalign-update}}\kappa^{2}}{c_{\lambda}\|X_{\star}\|}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta^{\prime}_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\frac{1}{2}\eta\|X_{\star}\|\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{7/12}.

(165)

Summing up (
138
), (
139
),
we obtain

\displaystyle{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Gamma_{t+1}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t+1}\widetilde{S}_{t+1}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{8}\right)({|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Gamma_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|})+\eta\frac{2(C_{\ref{lem:misalign-update}}+C_{\ref{lem:local-unify}}c_{\lambda})\kappa^{4}}{c_{\lambda}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta^{\prime}_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+2\eta\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{7/12}.

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{8}\right)({|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Gamma_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|})+\eta\frac{2(C_{\ref{lem:misalign-update}}+C_{\ref{lem:local-unify}}c_{\lambda})\kappa^{4}}{c_{\lambda}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}}({|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|})+2\eta\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{7/12}.

(166)

This is close to our desired conclusion,
but we would need to eliminate
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
.
To this end we shall need the following lemma.

Lemma 43
.

If
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\cdot|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
is taken to be the operator norm
\|\cdot\|
or the Frobenius norm
\|\cdot\|_{\fro}
, under the same setting as Lemma
42
, one has

{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}\leq 32c_{\delta}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left({|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\Gamma_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right).

(167)

Return to the proof of Lemma
42
. The conclusion follows from applying
the above lemma to the term
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
in (
166
),
noting that
c_{\delta}
can be chosen sufficiently small such that

\frac{2(C_{\ref{lem:misalign-update}}+C_{\ref{lem:local-unify}}c_{\lambda})}{c_{\lambda}}\cdot 32c_{\delta}<\frac{1}{16},

and that
\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|/\|X_{\star}\|

is sufficiently small due to (
24
), which still holds in the generalized setting in virtue of Lemma
32
.

G.3.2
Proof of Lemma
43

Observe that
U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}
has rank at most
r_{\star}
, thus

\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|\leq\|\Delta_{t}\|,\quad\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|_{\fro}\leq\sqrt{r_{\star}}\|\Delta_{t}\|.

On the other hand, from Lemma
12
, we know

\displaystyle\|{\Delta_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 8\delta\left(\|{\widetilde{S}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{\top}-\Sigma_{\star}^{2}}\|_{\fro}+\|\widetilde{S}_{t}\|\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}}\|_{\fro}+n\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 16c_{\delta}r_{\star}^{-1/2}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left(\|{\Gamma_{t}}\|_{\fro}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|_{\fro}+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 32c_{\delta}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left(\|{\Gamma_{t}}\|+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right)

where the penultimate line follows from (
10
)
and from controlling the sum inside the brackets
in a similar way as (
137
),
and the last line follows from
\Gamma_{t}=\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}\widetilde{S}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{\top}\Sigma_{\star}^{-1}-I
being a matrix of rank at most
r_{\star}+1
, which implies
\|\Gamma_{t}\|_{\fro}\leq\sqrt{r_{\star}+1}\|\Gamma_{t}\|
, and similarly
\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|_{\fro}\leq\sqrt{r_{\star}}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|
.
The conclusion then follows from bounding
\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|
and
\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|_{\fro}
separately. We have

\displaystyle\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|\leq\|\Delta_{t}\|\leq 32c_{\delta}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left(\|{\Gamma_{t}}\|+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right),

and

\displaystyle\|U_{\star}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|_{\fro}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r_{\star}}\|\Delta_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r_{\star}}\cdot 16c_{\delta}r_{\star}^{-1/2}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left(\|{\Gamma_{t}}\|_{\fro}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|_{\fro}+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right)

\displaystyle=16c_{\delta}\kappa^{-4}\|X_{\star}\|^{2}\left(\|{\Gamma_{t}}\|_{\fro}+\|X_{\star}\|^{-1}\|{\widetilde{N}_{t}\widetilde{S}_{t}^{-1}\Sigma_{\star}}\|_{\fro}+\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{O}_{t}\|}{\|X_{\star}\|}\right)^{2/3}\right).

Combining the above two inequalities together proves that (
167
) holds with
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\cdot|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
taken to be the operator norm or the Frobenius norm.

G.4
Proof of Theorem
6

Combining Lemma
32
, Lemma
38
and Lemma
40
, the final
t_{4}
given by Lemma
40

is no more than
4\times T_{\min}/16\leq T_{\min}/2
,
thus (
160
) holds for all
t\in[T_{\min}/2,T_{\max}]
,
in particular, for some
T\leq T_{\min}
.
Plugging in (
160
) the bound for
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|U_{\star}^{\top}E|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
given by Lemma
29
when
{|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|\cdot|\kern-1.07639pt|\kern-1.07639pt|}
is taken to be the operator norm
\|\cdot\|
or the Frobenius norm
\|\cdot\|_{\fro}
, we obtain the conclusion as desired.
